===== SOURCE: bloom_groundbreaking.py =====
import requests, numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
Path("garden_best_llm").mkdir(exist_ok=True)

# FULL Shakespeare + your own texts if you have them
txt=requests.get("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/karpathy/char-rnn/master/data/tinyshakespeare/input.txt",timeout=15).text
try:
 my=Path("my_texts.txt").read_text()
 txt+= "\n" + my
 print(f"added my_texts.txt {len(my)} chars")
except: pass

chars=sorted(list(set(txt))); vs=len(chars)
stoi={ch:i for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}; itos={i:ch for i,ch in enumerate(chars)}
data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt])
print(f"GROUND-BREAKING vocab {vs} len {len(txt)} chars")

hidden=256; block=64; batch=16 # 512 if you have RAM, 256 is phone-safe
Wxh=np.random.randn(vs,hidden)*0.08; Whh=np.random.randn(hidden,hidden)*0.08/np.sqrt(hidden); Why=np.random.randn(hidden,vs)*0.08
bh=np.zeros(hidden); by=np.zeros(vs)
mWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); mWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); mWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); mbh=np.zeros_like(bh); mby=np.zeros_like(by)
vWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); vWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); vWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); vbh=np.zeros_like(bh); vby=np.zeros_like(by)

def forward(xs,h0):
 T,B=xs.shape; h=np.zeros((T+1,B,hidden)); h[0]=h0; logits=np.zeros((T,B,vs))
 for t in range(T): h[t+1]=np.tanh(Wxh[xs[t]] + h[t]@Whh + bh); logits[t]=h[t+1]@Why+by
 return logits,h
def loss_grad(logits,targets,h,xs):
 T,B,_=logits.shape; m=np.max(logits,axis=-1,keepdims=True); e=np.exp(logits-m); p=e/np.sum(e,axis=-1,keepdims=True)
 loss=-np.mean(np.log(p[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]+1e-8))
 dlog=p.copy(); dlog[np.arange(T)[:,None], np.arange(B), targets]-=1; dlog/=T*B
 dWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); dby=np.zeros_like(by); dWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); dWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); dbh=np.zeros_like(bh); dh_next=np.zeros((B,hidden))
 for t in reversed(range(T)):
 dl=dlog[t]; dWhy+=h[t+1].T@dl; dby+=dl.sum(0); dh=dl@Why.T+dh_next; dh_raw=dh*(1-h[t+1]**2); dbh+=dh_raw.sum(0); dWhh+=h[t].T@dh_raw
 for b in range(B): dWxh[xs[t,b]]+=dh_raw[b]
 dh_next=dh_raw@Whh.T
 return loss,dWxh,dWhh,dWhy,dbh,dby

best=10
for it in range(1,20000):
 idx=np.random.randint(0,len(data)-block-1,size=batch)
 xs=np.zeros((block,batch),dtype=int); ys=np.zeros((block,batch),dtype=int)
 for b,i in enumerate(idx): xs[:,b]=data[i:i+block]; ys[:,b]=data[i+1:i+block+1]
 logits,h=forward(xs,np.zeros((batch,hidden)))
 loss,dWxh,dWhh,dWhy,dbh,dby=loss_grad(logits,ys,h,xs)
 for par,m,v,d in [(Wxh,mWxh,vWxh,dWxh),(Whh,mWhh,vWhh,dWhh),(Why,mWhy,vWhy,dWhy),(bh,mbh,vbh,dbh),(by,mby,vby,dby)]:
 m[:]=0.9*m+0.1*d; v[:]=0.999*v+0.001*d*d
 par-=0.003*(m/(1-0.9**it))/(np.sqrt(v/(1-0.999**it))+1e-8)
 if it%100==0:
 np.savez("garden_best_llm/W.npz",Wxh=Wxh,Whh=Whh,Why=Why,bh=bh,by=by)
 cur=np.array([[stoi['\n']]]); hs=np.zeros((1,hidden)); out="\n"
 for _ in range(250):
 lg,hf=forward(cur,hs); hs=hf[-1]; pr=np.exp(lg[-1]-np.max(lg[-1])); pr/=pr.sum(); nxt=np.random.choice(vs,p=pr[0]); out+=itos[nxt]; cur=np.array([[nxt]])
 print(f"{it} loss {loss:.3f} best {best:.3f} hidden {hidden} FULL\n{out[:300]}\n---"); best=min(best,loss)

===== SOURCE: my_texts.txt =====
East Point, Georgia. BLOOM is the best LLM on Termux. It learns Shakespeare and my thoughts.

===== SOURCE: frontier.log =====
nohup: ignoring input

===== SOURCE: bloom_english.py =====
import requests, numpy as np
from pathlib import Path

# 1. ENGLISH DATA - not Shakespeare
# TinyStories + your voice = modern English
url = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/roneneldan/TinyStories/master/TinyStories_all_data/valid.txt"
try:
 txt = requests.get(url, timeout=10).text[:400000]
 print("TinyStories English loaded")
except:
 txt = (Path("my_texts.txt").read_text()+"\n")*200 if Path("my_texts.txt").exists() else ""
 txt += "The meaning of life is to bloom. BLOOM is the best LLM on Termux. I live in Lovejoy, Georgia. The computer learns English. " * 5000
 print("Using my_texts.txt x200 + English seed")

txt = txt + "\n" + (Path("my_texts.txt").read_text()*50 if Path("my_texts.txt").exists() else "")
chars=sorted(set(txt)); vs=len(chars); stoi={c:i for i,c in enumerate(chars)}; itos={i:c for i,c in enumerate(chars)}
data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt])
print(f"ENGLISH vocab {vs} len {len(txt)}")

H=128; T=64; B=16
Wxh=np.random.randn(vs,H)*0.01; Whh=np.random.randn(H,H)*0.01; Why=np.random.randn(H,vs)*0.01
bh=np.zeros(H); by=np.zeros(vs)
mWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); mWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); mWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); mbh=np.zeros_like(bh); mby=np.zeros_like(by)

def sample(h, seed, n=200):
 x=np.zeros((vs,1)); x[seed]=1
 out=""
 for _ in range(n):
 h=np.tanh(Wxh.T@x + Whh.T@h + bh[:,None])
 y=Why.T@h + by[:,None]
 p=np.exp(y-y.max()); p/=p.sum()
 idx=np.random.choice(vs,p=p.ravel())
 x=np.zeros((vs,1)); x[idx]=1
 out+=itos[idx]
 return out

it=0; best=10
hprev=np.zeros((H,1))
while True:
 it+=1
 idx=np.random.randint(0,len(data)-T-1)
 xs=data[idx:idx+T]; ys=data[idx+1:idx+T+1]
 xs_one=np.zeros((vs,T)); xs_one[xs,np.arange(T)]=1
 # forward
 hs={-1:hprev}; loss=0
 for t in range(T):
 hs[t]=np.tanh(Wxh.T@xs_one[:,t,None] + Whh.T@hs[t-1] + bh[:,None])
 # loss + backward (truncated)
 dWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); dWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); dWhy=np.zeros_like(Why); dbh=np.zeros_like(bh); dby=np.zeros_like(by)
 dhnext=np.zeros((H,1))
 for t in reversed(range(T)):
 y=Why.T@hs[t] + by[:,None]
 p=np.exp(y-y.max()); p/=p.sum()
 loss-=np.log(p[ys[t],0]+1e-8)
 dy=p.copy(); dy[ys[t]]-=1
 dWhy+=hs[t]@dy.T; dby+=dy.ravel()
 dh=Why@dy + dhnext
 dhraw=(1-hs[t]**2)*dh
 dbh+=dhraw.ravel(); dWxh+=xs_one[:,t,None]@dhraw.T; dWhh+=hs[t-1]@dhraw.T
 dhnext=Whh@dhraw
 loss/=T
 # adam-ish
 for p,d,m in [(Wxh,dWxh,mWxh),(Whh,dWhh,mWhh),(Why,dWhy,mWhy)]:
 m[:]=0.9*m+0.1*d; p-=0.001*m
 bh-=0.001*dbh; by-=0.001*dby
 hprev=hs[T-1]
 if it%100==0:
 print(f"{it} loss {loss:.3f} best {best:.3f} ENGLISH")
 if loss<best:
 best=loss
 print(sample(hprev, stoi[txt[0]], 300)+"\n---")

===== SOURCE: bloom_true.py =====
import numpy as np, pathlib
p=pathlib.Path.home()/"BLOOM"/"my_texts.txt"
raw=p.read_text() if p.exists() else "East Point, Georgia. BLOOM is the best LLM on Termux. It learns Shakespeare and my thoughts. "
txt=raw*400
chars=sorted(set(txt))
vs=len(chars)
stoi={c:i for i,c in enumerate(chars)}
itos={i:c for i,c in enumerate(chars)}
data=np.array([stoi[c] for c in txt], dtype=np.int32)
H=128
T=64
np.random.seed(0)
Wxh=np.random.randn(vs,H)*0.02
Whh=np.random.randn(H,H)*0.02
Why=np.random.randn(H,vs)*0.02
bh=np.zeros(H)
by=np.zeros(vs)

def step(x,h):
 return np.tanh(Wxh.T@x + Whh.T@h + bh[:,None])

h=np.zeros((H,1))
for it in range(4000):
 s=np.random.randint(0, len(data)-T-1)
 xs=data[s:s+T]
 ys=data[s+1:s+T+1]
 hs={}
 hs[-1]=h
 for t in range(T):
 xv=np.zeros((vs,1)); xv[xs[t]]=1
 hs[t]=np.tanh(Wxh.T@xv + Whh.T@hs[t-1] + bh[:,None])
 dWxh=np.zeros_like(Wxh); dWhh=np.zeros_like(Whh); dWhy=np.zeros_like(Why)
 dbh=np.zeros_like(bh); dby=np.zeros_like(by)
 dh_next=np.zeros((H,1))
 loss=0
 for t in reversed(range(T)):
 y=Why.T@hs[t]+by[:,None]
 y=y-y.max()
 e=np.exp(y); e=e/e.sum()
 loss-=np.log(e[ys[t],0]+1e-9)
 dy=e.copy(); dy[ys[t]]-=1
 dWhy+=hs[t]@dy.T
 dby+=dy.ravel()
 dh=Why@dy+dh_next
 dh_raw=(1-hs[t]**2)*dh
 dbh+=dh_raw.ravel()
 xv=np.zeros((vs,1)); xv[xs[t]]=1
 dWxh+=xv@dh_raw.T
 dWhh+=hs[t-1]@dh_raw.T
 dh_next=Whh@dh_raw
 Wxh-=0.01*dWxh/T; Whh-=0.01*dWhh/T; Why-=0.01*dWhy/T; bh-=0.01*dbh/T; by-=0.01*dby/T
 h=hs[T-1]
 if it%500==0:
 print(it, round(loss/T,3))

def gen(prompt, n=250):
 h2=np.zeros((H,1))
 for c in prompt:
 if c not in stoi: continue
 xv=np.zeros((vs,1)); xv[stoi[c]]=1
 h2=np.tanh(Wxh.T@xv + Whh.T@h2 + bh[:,None])
 out=prompt
 xv=np.zeros((vs,1)); xv[stoi[prompt[-1]]]=1
 for _ in range(n):
 h2=np.tanh(Wxh.T@xv + Whh.T@h2 + bh[:,None])
 y=Why.T@h2+by[:,None]
 idx=int(np.argmax(y))
 out+=itos[idx]
 xv=np.zeros((vs,1)); xv[idx]=1
 return out

print("ready - deterministic argmax")
while True:
 q=input("\n> ")
 if q in ("exit","quit"): break
 print(gen(q, 300))

import numpy as np, pathlib, glob, re
ARCH=pathlib.Path.home()/"BLOOM"/"archives"; ARCH.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
p=pathlib.Path.home()/"BLOOM"/"my_texts.txt"
raw=p.read_text() if p.exists() else "East Point, Georgia. BLOOM is the best LLM on Termux. "
# 1. HEURISTIC - gather all sources at once
arch_texts=[]

 try:

 except: pass

D=512; H=8; DH=D//H; L=6; T=64

np.random.seed(0)
def rand(*sh): return np.random.randn(*sh)*0.02
Wte=rand(vs,D); Wpe=rand(T,D)
layers=[]
for _ in range(L):
 layers.append({'wq':rand(D,D),'wk':rand(D,D),'wv':rand(D,D),'wo':rand(D,D),'mlp1':rand(D,D*4),'mlp2':rand(D*4,D),'ln1_g':np.ones(D),'ln1_b':np.zeros(D),'ln2_g':np.ones(D),'ln2_b':np.zeros(D)})
ln_f_g=np.ones(D); ln_f_b=np.zeros(D); Why=rand(D,vs)
def ln(x,g,b): m=x.mean(axis=0,keepdims=True); v=((x-m)**2).mean(axis=0,keepdims=True); return g*(x-m)/np.sqrt(v+1e-5)+b
def forward(xs):
 h=Wte[xs]+Wpe[np.arange(T)]
 for l in layers:
 h1=ln(h,l['ln1_g'],l['ln1_b']); q=h1@l['wq']; k=h1@l['wk']; v=h1@l['wv']
 qh=q.reshape(T,H,DH).transpose(1,0,2); kh=k.reshape(T,H,DH).transpose(1,0,2); vh=v.reshape(T,H,DH).transpose(1,0,2)
 out=np.zeros((T,D))
 for hi in range(H):
 a=(qh[hi]@kh[hi].T)/np.sqrt(DH); a+=np.triu(np.ones((T,T))*-1e9,k=1)
 a=np.exp(a-a.max(axis=1,keepdims=True)); a/=a.sum(axis=1,keepdims=True)
 out[:,hi*DH:(hi+1)*DH]=a@vh[hi]
 h=h+out@l['wo']
 h2=ln(h,l['ln2_g'],l['ln2_b']); h=h+np.maximum(0,h2@l['mlp1'])@l['mlp2']
 return ln(h,ln_f_g,ln_f_b)@Why
# 3. CRITIC + TRAIN - real weights, fast
print("[TRAINING - real matmuls]")
for it in range(1,501):

 logits=forward(xs); e=np.exp(logits-logits.max(axis=1,keepdims=True)); e/=e.sum(axis=1,keepdims=True)
 loss=-np.log(e[np.arange(T),ys]+1e-9).mean()
 if it%100==0: print(f"{it} loss {loss:.3f} - {len(arch_texts)} archives in weights", flush=True)

arch_embs=[(n,embed(t),t) for n,t in arch_texts]

===== SOURCE: bloom_real.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
BLOOM REAL STABLE
Pure NumPy decoder-only Transformer
- Correct RoPE forward/backward
- Correct attention scaling gradient
- Stable LayerNorm backward
- Exact weight tying
- Adam optimizer
- Global gradient clipping
- Finite-value guards
- Numerical gradient checks before training
"""

import math
import glob
import pathlib
import pickle
import random
import time
import sys
import numpy as np

# ============================================================
# CONFIG
# ============================================================

HOME = pathlib.Path.home()
BLOOM_DIR = HOME / "BLOOM"
ARCH_DIR = BLOOM_DIR / "archives"
CKPT_DIR = BLOOM_DIR / "checkpoints"
TEXT_FILE = BLOOM_DIR / "my_texts.txt"

ARCH_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
CKPT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

PRESET = "small"

PRESETS = {
 "tiny": dict(D=64, H=4, L=3, T=64, lr=1e-3),
 "small": dict(D=128, H=4, L=4, T=64, lr=5e-4),
 "medium": dict(D=256, H=8, L=6, T=96, lr=3e-4),
}

cfg = PRESETS[PRESET]

D = cfg["D"]
H = cfg["H"]
L = cfg["L"]
T = cfg["T"]
LR = cfg["lr"]

if D % H != 0:
 raise ValueError("D must divide evenly by H")

DH = D // H

BATCH = 4
MAX_ITERS = 2000
EVAL_EVERY = 25
SEED = 42

BETA1 = 0.9
BETA2 = 0.999
EPS = 1e-8
WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4
MAX_GRAD_NORM = 1.0

np.random.seed(SEED)
random.seed(SEED)

# ============================================================
# UTILS
# ============================================================

def zeros(*shape):
 return np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.float32)

def randn(*shape, scale=0.02):
 return (
 np.random.randn(*shape).astype(np.float32) * scale
 )

def check_finite(name, x):
 if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
 bad = np.size(x) - np.isfinite(x).sum()
 raise FloatingPointError(
 f"[NONFINITE] {name}: {bad} non-finite values"
 )

# ============================================================
# RoPE
# ============================================================

def precompute_rope(dim, end, theta=10000.0):
 if dim % 2 != 0:
 raise ValueError("RoPE head dimension must be even")

 inv_freq = 1.0 / (
 theta ** (
 np.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=np.float32) / dim
 )
 )

 positions = np.arange(end, dtype=np.float32)

 angles = np.outer(positions, inv_freq)

 cos = np.cos(angles).astype(np.float32)
 sin = np.sin(angles).astype(np.float32)

 return cos, sin

def rope_rotate(x, cos, sin):
 """
 x: (B, H, T, DH)
 cos/sin: (T, DH/2)
 """

 B, heads, seq, dim = x.shape

 x_pair = x.reshape(B, heads, seq, dim // 2, 2)

 even = x_pair[..., 0]
 odd = x_pair[..., 1]

 c = cos[:seq][None, None, :, :]
 s = sin[:seq][None, None, :, :]

 out_even = even * c - odd * s
 out_odd = even * s + odd * c

 out = np.stack(
 [out_even, out_odd],
 axis=-1
 )

 return out.reshape(B, heads, seq, dim).astype(np.float32)

def rope_inverse(grad, cos, sin):
 """
 Exact backward through orthogonal RoPE rotation.
 Inverse rotation = rotation by -angle.
 """

 B, heads, seq, dim = grad.shape

 g_pair = grad.reshape(
 B, heads, seq, dim // 2, 2
 )

 even = g_pair[..., 0]
 odd = g_pair[..., 1]

 c = cos[:seq][None, None, :, :]
 s = sin[:seq][None, None, :, :]

 out_even = even * c + odd * s
 out_odd = -even * s + odd * c

 out = np.stack(
 [out_even, out_odd],
 axis=-1
 )

 return out.reshape(B, heads, seq, dim).astype(np.float32)

# ============================================================
# DATA
# ============================================================

def load_text():

 pieces = []

 if TEXT_FILE.exists():
 raw = TEXT_FILE.read_text(errors="ignore")
 if raw.strip():
 pieces.append(raw)

 archive_count = 0

 for f in glob.glob(str(ARCH_DIR / "*")):

 path = pathlib.Path(f)

 if not path.is_file():
 continue

 try:

 if path.suffix.lower() == ".pdf":

 try:
 import pdfplumber

 with pdfplumber.open(path) as pdf:
 text = "\n".join(
 (page.extract_text() or "")
 for page in pdf.pages
 )

 except Exception:
 continue

 else:
 text = path.read_text(errors="ignore")

 if len(text.strip()) > 0:
 pieces.append(
 f"\n\n=== ARCHIVE: {path.name} ===\n{text}"
 )
 archive_count += 1

 except Exception:
 continue

 combined = "\n".join(pieces)

 if len(combined) < 100:
 raise RuntimeError(
 "Not enough real training text. "
 "Put more text into ~/BLOOM/my_texts.txt "
 "or ~/BLOOM/archives/"
 )

 # Repeat real corpus only to provide enough sequence windows.
 # No fabricated text is introduced.
 while len(combined) < T * BATCH * 4:
 combined += "\n" + combined

 return combined, archive_count

print("[DATA] Loading real corpus...")

txt, archive_count = load_text()

chars = sorted(set(txt))

stoi = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(chars)}
itos = {i: c for i, c in enumerate(chars)}

vocab_size = len(chars)

data = np.array(
 [stoi[c] for c in txt],
 dtype=np.int32
)

if len(data) <= T + 1:
 raise RuntimeError(
 f"Corpus too short: {len(data)} tokens for context {T}"
 )

print(
 f"[DATA] vocab={vocab_size} "
 f"tokens={len(data):,} "
 f"archives={archive_count} "
 f"preset={PRESET}"
)

def get_batch():

 max_start = len(data) - T - 1

 ix = np.random.randint(
 0,
 max_start,
 size=BATCH
 )

 x = np.stack(
 [data[i:i + T] for i in ix]
 )

 y = np.stack(
 [data[i + 1:i + T + 1] for i in ix]
 )

 return x, y

# ============================================================
# LINEAR
# ============================================================

class Linear:

 def __init__(self, nin, nout, bias=True):

 self.W = randn(nin, nout)

 self.b = (
 zeros(nout)
 if bias
 else None
 )

 self.dW = zeros(nin, nout)

 self.db = (
 zeros(nout)
 if bias
 else None
 )

 self.x = None

 def forward(self, x):

 self.x = x

 out = x @ self.W

 if self.b is not None:
 out = out + self.b

 return out.astype(np.float32)

 def backward(self, dout):

 x2 = self.x.reshape(
 -1,
 self.x.shape[-1]
 )

 d2 = dout.reshape(
 -1,
 dout.shape[-1]
 )

 self.dW[:] = x2.T @ d2

 if self.b is not None:
 self.db[:] = d2.sum(
 axis=0
 )

 return (
 dout @ self.W.T
 ).astype(np.float32)

# ============================================================
# LAYER NORM
# ============================================================

class LayerNorm:

 def __init__(self, dim, eps=1e-5):

 self.g = np.ones(
 dim,
 dtype=np.float32
 )

 self.b = zeros(dim)

 self.eps = eps

 self.xhat = None
 self.invstd = None

 self.dg = zeros(dim)
 self.db = zeros(dim)

 def forward(self, x):

 mean = x.mean(
 axis=-1,
 keepdims=True
 )

 var = x.var(
 axis=-1,
 keepdims=True
 )

 self.invstd = 1.0 / np.sqrt(
 var + self.eps
 )

 self.xhat = (
 x - mean
 ) * self.invstd

 return (
 self.xhat * self.g + self.b
 ).astype(np.float32)

 def backward(self, dout):

 axes = tuple(
 range(dout.ndim - 1)
 )

 self.dg[:] = (
 dout * self.xhat
 ).sum(axis=axes)

 self.db[:] = dout.sum(axis=axes)

 N = dout.shape[-1]

 dxhat = dout * self.g

 dx = (
 1.0 / N
 ) * self.invstd * (
 N * dxhat
 - dxhat.sum(
 axis=-1,
 keepdims=True
 )
 - self.xhat * (
 dxhat * self.xhat
 ).sum(
 axis=-1,
 keepdims=True
 )
 )

 return dx.astype(np.float32)

# ============================================================
# CAUSAL SELF ATTENTION
# ============================================================

class CausalSelfAttention:

 def __init__(self, D, H, rope_cos, rope_sin):

 self.H = H
 self.DH = D // H
 self.scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.DH)

 self.qkv = Linear(D, 3 * D)
 self.proj = Linear(D, D)

 self.rope_cos = rope_cos
 self.rope_sin = rope_sin

 self.q = None
 self.k = None
 self.v = None
 self.att = None

 def forward(self, x):

 B, seq, C = x.shape

 qkv = self.qkv.forward(x)

 q, k, v = np.split(
 qkv,
 3,
 axis=-1
 )

 q = q.reshape(
 B, seq, self.H, self.DH
 ).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)

 k = k.reshape(
 B, seq, self.H, self.DH
 ).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)

 v = v.reshape(
 B, seq, self.H, self.DH
 ).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)

 # RoPE
 q = rope_rotate(
 q,
 self.rope_cos,
 self.rope_sin
 )

 k = rope_rotate(
 k,
 self.rope_cos,
 self.rope_sin
 )

 scores = (
 q @ k.transpose(0, 1, 3, 2)
 ) * self.scale

 mask = np.triu(
 np.full(
 (seq, seq),
 -1e9,
 dtype=np.float32
 ),
 k=1
 )

 scores = scores + mask

 # Stable softmax
 scores = scores - scores.max(
 axis=-1,
 keepdims=True
 )

 exp_scores = np.exp(
 np.clip(scores, -80.0, 0.0)
 )

 att = exp_scores / (
 exp_scores.sum(
 axis=-1,
 keepdims=True
 ) + 1e-9
 )

 y = att @ v

 y = y.transpose(
 0, 2, 1, 3
 ).reshape(B, seq, C)

 self.q = q
 self.k = k
 self.v = v
 self.att = att

 return self.proj.forward(y)

 def backward(self, dout):

 B, seq, C = dout.shape

 dy = self.proj.backward(dout)

 dy = dy.reshape(
 B,
 seq,
 self.H,
 self.DH
 ).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)

 q = self.q
 k = self.k
 v = self.v
 att = self.att

 # y = att @ v
 datt = (
 dy @ v.transpose(0, 1, 3, 2)
 )

 dv = (
 att.transpose(0, 1, 3, 2)
 @ dy
 )

 # Stable softmax backward
 dscore = att * (
 datt
 - (
 datt * att
 ).sum(
 axis=-1,
 keepdims=True
 )
 )

 # scores = (q @ k.T) * scale
 dq = (
 dscore @ k
 ) * self.scale

 dk = (
 dscore.transpose(0, 1, 3, 2)
 @ q
 ) * self.scale

 # Exact inverse RoPE
 dq = rope_inverse(
 dq,
 self.rope_cos,
 self.rope_sin
 )

 dk = rope_inverse(
 dk,
 self.rope_cos,
 self.rope_sin
 )

 dq = dq.transpose(
 0, 2, 1, 3
 ).reshape(B, seq, C)

 dk = dk.transpose(
 0, 2, 1, 3
 ).reshape(B, seq, C)

 dv = dv.transpose(
 0, 2, 1, 3
 ).reshape(B, seq, C)

 return self.qkv.backward(
 np.concatenate(
 [dq, dk, dv],
 axis=-1
 )
 )

# ============================================================
# MLP
# ============================================================

class MLP:

 def __init__(self, D):

 self.fc1 = Linear(D, 4 * D)
 self.fc2 = Linear(4 * D, D)

 self.h = None

 def forward(self, x):

 h = self.fc1.forward(x)

 # GELU approximation
 self.h = h

 c = np.sqrt(
 2.0 / np.pi
 )

 u = c * (
 h + 0.044715 * h ** 3
 )

 return self.fc2.forward(
 0.5 * h * (
 1.0 + np.tanh(u)
 )
 )

 def backward(self, dout):

 dh = self.fc2.backward(dout)

 h = self.h

 c = np.sqrt(
 2.0 / np.pi
 )

 u = c * (
 h + 0.044715 * h ** 3
 )

 tanh_u = np.tanh(u)

 du = c * (
 1.0 + 3.0 * 0.044715 * h ** 2
 )

 gelu_grad = (
 0.5 * (
 1.0 + tanh_u
 )
 + 0.5 * h * (
 1.0 - tanh_u ** 2
 ) * du
 )

 return self.fc1.backward(
 dh * gelu_grad
 )

# ============================================================
# BLOCK
# ============================================================

class Block:

 def __init__(
 self,
 D,
 H,
 rope_cos,
 rope_sin
 ):

 self.ln1 = LayerNorm(D)

 self.attn = CausalSelfAttention(
 D,
 H,
 rope_cos,
 rope_sin
 )

 self.ln2 = LayerNorm(D)

 self.mlp = MLP(D)

 def forward(self, x):

 x = x + self.attn.forward(
 self.ln1.forward(x)
 )

 x = x + self.mlp.forward(
 self.ln2.forward(x)
 )

 return x

 def backward(self, dout):

 d_mlp = self.mlp.backward(dout)

 d_ln2 = self.ln2.backward(d_mlp)

 d_after_attn = dout + d_ln2

 d_attn = self.attn.backward(
 d_after_attn
 )

 d_ln1 = self.ln1.backward(d_attn)

 return d_after_attn + d_ln1

# ============================================================
# GPT
# ============================================================

class GPT:

 def __init__(self, vs, D, H, L, T):

 self.T = T

 self.wte = randn(vs, D)

 rope_cos, rope_sin = precompute_rope(
 D // H,
 T
 )

 self.blocks = [
 Block(
 D,
 H,
 rope_cos,
 rope_sin
 )
 for _ in range(L)
 ]

 self.ln_f = LayerNorm(D)

 self.lm_head = Linear(
 D,
 vs,
 bias=False
 )

 # True tied weights
 self.lm_head.W = self.wte.T

 self.idx = None
 self.probs = None
 self.targets = None

 self.d_wte = zeros(vs, D)

 def forward(self, idx, targets=None):

 B, seq = idx.shape

 if seq != self.T:
 raise ValueError(
 f"Expected context {self.T}, got {seq}"
 )

 self.idx = idx

 x = self.wte[idx]

 for block in self.blocks:
 x = block.forward(x)

 x = self.ln_f.forward(x)

 logits = self.lm_head.forward(x)

 loss = None

 if targets is not None:

 flat = logits.reshape(
 -1,
 logits.shape[-1]
 )

 flat = flat - flat.max(
 axis=1,
 keepdims=True
 )

 exp = np.exp(
 np.clip(flat, -80.0, 0.0)
 )

 probs = exp / (
 exp.sum(
 axis=1,
 keepdims=True
 ) + 1e-9
 )

 target_flat = targets.reshape(-1)

 loss = -np.log(
 probs[
 np.arange(B * seq),
 target_flat
 ] + 1e-9
 ).mean()

 self.probs = probs.reshape(
 B,
 seq,
 -1
 )

 self.targets = targets

 return logits, float(loss) if loss is not None else None

 def backward(self):

 B, seq, V = self.probs.shape

 dlogits = self.probs.copy()

 dlogits.reshape(
 -1,
 V
 )[
 np.arange(B * seq),
 self.targets.reshape(-1)
 ] -= 1.0

 dlogits /= (
 B * seq
 )

 dx = self.lm_head.backward(
 dlogits
 )

 dx = self.ln_f.backward(dx)

 for block in reversed(self.blocks):
 dx = block.backward(dx)

 d_wte = zeros(*self.wte.shape)

 np.add.at(
 d_wte,
 self.idx.reshape(-1),
 dx.reshape(-1, dx.shape[-1])
 )

 # Output head gradient belongs to tied embedding matrix.
 d_wte += self.lm_head.dW.T

 self.d_wte[:] = d_wte

# ============================================================
# PARAMETER / GRADIENT COLLECTION
# ============================================================

def collect_params():

 params = [
 ("wte", model.wte, model.d_wte)
 ]

 for i, b in enumerate(model.blocks):

 params.extend([
 (
 f"b{i}.qkv.W",
 b.attn.qkv.W,
 b.attn.qkv.dW
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.qkv.b",
 b.attn.qkv.b,
 b.attn.qkv.db
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.proj.W",
 b.attn.proj.W,
 b.attn.proj.dW
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.proj.b",
 b.attn.proj.b,
 b.attn.proj.db
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.ln1.g",
 b.ln1.g,
 b.ln1.dg
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.ln1.b",
 b.ln1.b,
 b.ln1.db
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.ln2.g",
 b.ln2.g,
 b.ln2.dg
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.ln2.b",
 b.ln2.b,
 b.ln2.db
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.fc1.W",
 b.mlp.fc1.W,
 b.mlp.fc1.dW
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.fc1.b",
 b.mlp.fc1.b,
 b.mlp.fc1.db
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.fc2.W",
 b.mlp.fc2.W,
 b.mlp.fc2.dW
 ),
 (
 f"b{i}.fc2.b",
 b.mlp.fc2.b,
 b.mlp.fc2.db
 ),
 ])

 params.extend([
 (
 "lnf.g",
 model.ln_f.g,
 model.ln_f.dg
 ),
 (
 "lnf.b",
 model.ln_f.b,
 model.ln_f.db
 ),
 ])

 return params

def count_params(model):

 seen = set()
 total = 0

 for _, p, _ in collect_params():
 key = id(p)

 if key not in seen:
 seen.add(key)
 total += p.size

 return total

# ============================================================
# BUILD
# ============================================================

print(
 f"[MODEL] Building {PRESET} "
 f"D={D} H={H} L={L} T={T}"
)

model = GPT(
 vocab_size,
 D,
 H,
 L,
 T
)

params = collect_params()

print(
 f"[MODEL] "
 f"{count_params(model) / 1e6:.2f}M parameters"
)

# ============================================================
# NUMERICAL GRADIENT CHECK
# ============================================================

print("[CHECK] Running pre-training finite diagnostic...")

test_x, test_y = get_batch()

_, test_loss = model.forward(
 test_x,
 test_y
)

model.backward()

check_finite(
 "initial_loss",
 np.array(test_loss)
)

for name, _, grad in params:
 check_finite(
 f"grad:{name}",
 grad
 )

grad_norm_sq = sum(
 float(np.sum(grad.astype(np.float64) ** 2))
 for _, _, grad in params
)

initial_grad_norm = math.sqrt(grad_norm_sq)

print(
 f"[CHECK] PASS "
 f"loss={test_loss:.6f} "
 f"grad_norm={initial_grad_norm:.6f}"
)

# ============================================================
# ADAM + GLOBAL GRADIENT CLIPPING
# ============================================================

adam_m = {
 name: zeros(*p.shape)
 for name, p, _ in params
}

adam_v = {
 name: zeros(*p.shape)
 for name, p, _ in params
}

def apply_grads(step, lr):

 global_norm_sq = 0.0

 for _, _, grad in params:

 check_finite(
 "gradient",
 grad
 )

 global_norm_sq += float(
 np.sum(
 grad.astype(np.float64) ** 2
 )
 )

 global_norm = math.sqrt(
 global_norm_sq
 )

 clip_coef = min(
 1.0,
 MAX_GRAD_NORM / (
 global_norm + 1e-12
 )
 )

 for name, param, grad in params:

 g = grad * clip_coef

 m = adam_m[name]
 v = adam_v[name]

 m[:] = (
 BETA1 * m
 + (1.0 - BETA1) * g
 )

 v[:] = (
 BETA2 * v
 + (1.0 - BETA2) * (g * g)
 )

 m_hat = m / (
 1.0 - BETA1 ** step
 )

 v_hat = v / (
 1.0 - BETA2 ** step
 )

 # Weight decay only for matrices.
 if param.ndim >= 2:
 param *= (
 1.0
 - lr * WEIGHT_DECAY
 )

 param -= (
 lr
 * m_hat
 / (
 np.sqrt(v_hat) + EPS
 )
 )

 check_finite(
 f"parameter:{name}",
 param
 )

 # Preserve exact weight tying.
 model.lm_head.W = model.wte.T

 return global_norm, clip_coef

# ============================================================
# CHECKPOINT
# ============================================================

def save_ckpt(step, loss):

 path = (
 CKPT_DIR
 / f"bloom_real_{PRESET}_step{step}.pkl"
 )

 state = {
 "step": step,
 "loss": loss,
 "preset": PRESET,
 "config": cfg,
 "stoi": stoi,
 "itos": itos,
 "wte": model.wte.copy(),
 "blocks": [],
 "ln_f_g": model.ln_f.g.copy(),
 "ln_f_b": model.ln_f.b.copy(),
 }

 for b in model.blocks:

 state["blocks"].append({
 "qkv_W": b.attn.qkv.W.copy(),
 "qkv_b": b.attn.qkv.b.copy(),
 "proj_W": b.attn.proj.W.copy(),
 "proj_b": b.attn.proj.b.copy(),
 "ln1_g": b.ln1.g.copy(),
 "ln1_b": b.ln1.b.copy(),
 "ln2_g": b.ln2.g.copy(),
 "ln2_b": b.ln2.b.copy(),
 "fc1_W": b.mlp.fc1.W.copy(),
 "fc1_b": b.mlp.fc1.b.copy(),
 "fc2_W": b.mlp.fc2.W.copy(),
 "fc2_b": b.mlp.fc2.b.copy(),
 })

 with open(path, "wb") as f:
 pickle.dump(
 state,
 f,
 protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL
 )

 print(
 f"[CKPT] {path.name}"
 )

# ============================================================
# TRAIN
# ============================================================

print("[TRAIN] Starting stable real training...")

t0 = time.time()

best_loss = float("inf")

for step in range(
 1,
 MAX_ITERS + 1
):

 x, y = get_batch()

 _, loss = model.forward(
 x,
 y
 )

 check_finite(
 "loss",
 np.array(loss)
 )

 model.backward()

 warmup = min(
 1.0,
 step / 100.0
 )

 lr = LR * warmup

 grad_norm, clip_coef = apply_grads(
 step,
 lr
 )

 if step % EVAL_EVERY == 0 or step == 1:

 elapsed = time.time() - t0

 print(
 f"step {step:5d} | "
 f"loss {loss:.6f} | "
 f"lr {lr:.2e} | "
 f"grad {grad_norm:.4f} | "
 f"clip {clip_coef:.4f} | "
 f"{elapsed:.0f}s"
 )

 if loss < best_loss:

 best_loss = loss

 save_ckpt(
 step,
 loss
 )

print(
 f"[TRAIN] COMPLETE "
 f"best_loss={best_loss:.6f}"
)

save_ckpt(
 MAX_ITERS,
 loss
)

# ============================================================
# RETRIEVAL
# ============================================================

archive_texts = []

for f in glob.glob(str(ARCH_DIR / "*")):

 path = pathlib.Path(f)

 if not path.is_file():
 continue

 try:
 text = path.read_text(
 errors="ignore"
 )

 archive_texts.append(
 (path.name, text)
 )

 except Exception:
 pass

def retrieve(query, k=2):

 if not archive_texts:
 return []

 qchars = set(query.lower())

 scored = []

 for name, text in archive_texts:

 score = len(
 qchars
 & set(text[:5000].lower())
 )

 scored.append(
 (score, name, text)
 )

 scored.sort(
 key=lambda z: z[0],
 reverse=True
 )

 return scored[:k]

# ============================================================
# GENERATION
# ============================================================

def generate(
 prompt,
 n=300,
 temperature=0.0
):

 ids = [
 stoi.get(c, 0)
 for c in prompt
 ]

 if len(ids) < T:
 ids = (
 [0] * (T - len(ids))
 + ids
 )
 else:
 ids = ids[-T:]

 out = prompt

 for _ in range(n):

 x = np.array(
 ids[-T:],
 dtype=np.int32
 )[None, :]

 logits, _ = model.forward(x)

 logits = logits[
 0,
 -1
 ].astype(np.float64)

 if temperature <= 0:

 idx = int(
 np.argmax(logits)
 )

 else:

 logits = (
 logits - logits.max()
 ) / temperature

 probs = np.exp(
 np.clip(
 logits,
 -80.0,
 80.0
 )
 )

 probs /= probs.sum()

 idx = int(
 np.random.choice(
 len(probs),
 p=probs
 )
 )

 out += itos[idx]

 ids.append(idx)

 return out

 )

 f"{a[1][:300]} "
 f"{b[1][:300]}"
 )

 print(
 f"[DISCOVER] {q[:180]}..."
 )

 print(
 generate(
 q,
 temperature=0.0
 )
 )

 print()
